#### 配置容器

本模块包含 **31** 个配置容器：

##### 1. PduRBswModules

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `6e61fc0a-7f1d-45fa-b350-430e6fea3c7b`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRBswModules`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Each container describes a specific BSW module  that the PDU Router shall interface to. All interfacing modules (below and above the PDUR) including their API requirements need to be configured explicitly.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **11** 个参数：

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. PduRCancelReceive

- **UUID**: `7e7cce1b-2d46-48e0-8218-9a7126a35902`
- **参数名**: `PduRCancelReceive`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the transport protocol BSW module supports the CancelReceive API or not. The API allows to cancel an ongoing transport protocol reception.

Value true means that the BSW module supports the CancelReceive interface which an upper layer module can call and also that it can call the CancelReceive interface of a lower layer module.
Value false means that the BSW module does not support the CancelReceive interface which an upper layer module can call and also that it shall not call the CancelReceive interface of a lower layer module.

######## 2. PduRCancelTransmit

- **UUID**: `1b383d4a-1079-4f46-bfdf-40b3a6bb0d3d`
- **参数名**: `PduRCancelTransmit`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the BSW module supports the CancelTransmit API or not. The API allows to cancel an ongoing transport protocol transmission.

Value true means that the BSW module supports the CancelTransmit interface which an upper layer module can call and also that it can call the CancelTransmit interface of a lower layer module.
Value false means that the BSW module does not support the CancelTransmit interface which an upper layer module can call and also that it shall not call the CancelTransmit interface of a lower layer module.

######## 3. PduRChangeParameterRequestApi

- **UUID**: `8b0e626d-8a3f-468f-ac06-fdb42825ee87`
- **参数名**: `PduRChangeParameterRequestApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the transport protocol BSW module supports the ChangeParameter API or not. The API allows to change transport protocol parameters like BS or STmin.

Value true means that the BSW module supports the ChangeParameter interface which an upper layer module can call and also that it can call the ChangeParameter interface of a lower layer module.
Value false means that the BSW module does not support the ChangeParameter interface which an upper layer module can call and also that it shall not call the ChangeParameter interface of a lower layer module.

######## 4. PduRCommunicationInterface

- **UUID**: `f76c3d09-53d6-4df0-84dc-5c6e422a2a09`
- **参数名**: `PduRCommunicationInterface`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the BSW module supports the communication interface APIs or not. A module can have both APIs, communication interface and transport protocol (e.g. the Com module).

Value true means that the BSW module supports the communication interface APIs.
Value false means that the BSW module does not support the communication interface APIs.

######## 5. PduRLowerModule

- **UUID**: `d5cc4dde-918c-423c-8caf-4fcbd7e9f23a`
- **参数名**: `PduRLowerModule`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Specifies who will call the BSW module APIs and who will implement the called APIs. An upper module can also be an lower module (e.g. the IpduM module).

For example, if the CanIf module is referenced then the PduR module will implement the PduR_CanIfRxIndication API. And the PduR module will call the CanIf_Transmit API.

######## 6. PduRRetransmission

- **UUID**: `696b9226-5d9d-427e-8967-4448297e7b61`
- **参数名**: `PduRRetransmission`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..0]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is not used by the PduR. This featue is not supported.

Specifies if the destination transport protocol BSW module supports the the retransmission feature. This parameter might be set to false if the retransmission feature is not used, even though the destination transport protocol is supporting it.

######## 7. PduRTransportProtocol

- **UUID**: `636d0921-54b3-4e04-80b2-43468dd180d6`
- **参数名**: `PduRTransportProtocol`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the BSW module supports the transport protocol APIs or not.

Value true means that the BSW module supports the transport protocol APIs.
Value false means that the BSW module does not support the transport protocol APIs.

######## 8. PduRTriggertransmit

- **UUID**: `21a4f51b-3cce-4bbd-af24-45e3c7d7e218`
- **参数名**: `PduRTriggertransmit`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the BSW module supports the TriggerTransmit API or not.

Value true means that the BSW module supports the TriggerTransmit interface which a lower layer module can call and also that it can call the TriggerTransmit interface of an upper layer module.
Value false means that the BSW module does not support the TriggerTransmit interface which a lower layer module can call and also that it shall not call the TriggerTransmit interface of an upper layer module.

######## 9. PduRTxConfirmation

- **UUID**: `052164fc-d8f6-4b22-8c61-7159fc5f4641`
- **参数名**: `PduRTxConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the transport protocol BSW module supports the TxConfirmation API or not.

Value true means that the BSW module supports the TxConfirmation interface which a lower layer module can call and also that it can call the TxConfirmation interface of an upper layer module.
Value false means that the BSW module does not support the TxConfirmation interface which a lower layer module can call and also that it shall not call the TxConfirmation interface of an upper layer module.

######## 10. PduRUpperModule

- **UUID**: `85ab5e55-3db3-471e-95b4-990f15ec338a`
- **参数名**: `PduRUpperModule`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Specifies who will call the BSW module APIs and who will implement the called APIs. An upper module can also be an lower module (e.g. the IpduM module).

For example, if the Com module is referenced then the PduR module will implement the PduR_ComTransmit API. And the PduR module will call the Com_RxIndication API. Other APIs are of course also covered.

######## 11. PduRUseTag

- **UUID**: `ec686279-9792-44a1-8759-fa89cd85cb19`
- **参数名**: `PduRUseTag`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: True enables the usage of the tag "<Up>" in the following API calls:
- PduR_<Up>CancelReceive
- PduR_<Up>CancelTransmit
- PduR_<Up>ChangeParameter

Example: If used by Com and the parameter is enabled the PduR_ComCancelTransmit is used. The background is that upper layer modules differ in usage of this tag (e.g. Com is using the tag, Dcm is not).

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. PduRDeferredEventCacheSize

- **UUID**: `6b625175-959d-478c-a247-db1ec34ee29d`
- **参数名**: `PduRDeferredEventCacheSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Size of the Deferred Event Cache. It caches the handle Ids of Deferred routing paths which then will be processed in the next MainFunction.
The size is used per Core. The size corresponds to the amount of deferred events which can be cached.

######## 2. PduRSpinlockRetryCounter

- **UUID**: `861a9e85-7cc4-45cc-b374-6ffa48d7c27d`
- **参数名**: `PduRSpinlockRetryCounter`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `4294967295`
- **取值范围**: [1..4294967295]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: After this value is reached, the Spinlock is assumed to be aquired even if the TryToGetSpinlock API says otherwise.

This value is ignored if there are no Spinlocks configured.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. PduRDevErrorDetect

- **UUID**: `2d7a794c-ecac-413d-a5c4-308c55de3a2a`
- **参数名**: `PduRDevErrorDetect`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter switches the Development Error Detection and Notification ON or OFF.
true: Development error detection is enabled.
false: Development error detection is disabled.

Note: In general, the development error detection is recommended during pre-test phase. It is not recommended to enable the development error detection in production code due to increased runtime and ROM needs.

######## 4. PduRMetaDataSupport

- **UUID**: `4a4cc468-2646-4187-b85d-1a1c6804fc32`
- **参数名**: `PduRMetaDataSupport`

######## 5. PduRPduCallback

- **UUID**: `97956065-7846-42f6-8b6b-fbfba39cb575`
- **参数名**: `PduRPduCallback`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates / deactivates Pdu callbacks.
The activation of routing path specific BswM callbacks is done by BswM mode request source references.

######## 6. PduRSwitching

- **UUID**: `f83c673d-042e-4deb-b01f-7760310147d0`
- **参数名**: `PduRSwitching`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The PduR switching functionality can be used to dynamically learn the destination of routing paths. This will be done using the contents of the Pdu metadata. For a detailed description of this feature see the PduR technical reference.
This parameter activates / deactivates the switching functionality.
The configuration of the switching behavior is done by the PduRDataPlane configuration structure.

######## 7. PduR_QueueOverflowNotification

- **UUID**: `b8659c3e-c68c-4765-9cb1-1b8649b1f455`
- **参数名**: `PduR_QueueOverflowNotification`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates / deactivates a PduR InterfaceTxBuffer overflow notification. The PduR_ErrorNotification.h and _PduR_ErrorNotification.c (the C- file is a template) is generated by the PduR if the "PduR Queue Overflow Notification" is enabled. This header must be included and the function must be implemented.

Backround: In case of a communication interface routing with unfavourable FIFO configuration or if the destination bus is not available a buffer overflow can occurs. In this case the FIFO is flushed.

Caution: The function is called in the interrupt context

- **描述**: Selects if safety checks are used. These checks improve robustness as e.g. invalid parameters are checked before function is executed.
This attribute has to be enabled for safety projects where this component is mapped to an ASIL partition.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **18** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. PduRReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold

- **UUID**: `010a6ce0-f683-4809-8ad9-c7300bf4de03`
- **参数名**: `PduRReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using the negation operator.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

Code: the code size is increased due to the usage of the negation operator in the data access.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is minimized.
Runtime: the runtime is increased due to the usage of the negation operator in the data access.

########## 2. PduRReduceBoolDataByNumericalComparisonThreshold

- **UUID**: `597de7e9-6c6d-4dde-b895-808ff2e5d0c9`
- **参数名**: `PduRReduceBoolDataByNumericalComparisonThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using numerical comparison with other ROM data.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

Code: the code size is increased due to the usage of the operation in the data access.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is minimized.
Runtime: the runtime is increased due to the usage of the operation in the data access.

########## 4. PduRReduceNumericalDataByOffsetThreshold

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. PduROutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer

- **UUID**: `2c2d8fc8-c0e4-4431-8b25-99a84feafce8`
- **参数名**: `PduROutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds write problems.
This debugging feature must not be used in production code!

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

########## 2. PduROutOfBoundsReadSanitizer

- **UUID**: `633c2722-7d98-4b15-8919-0db44a2af154`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds read problems.
This debugging feature must not be used in production code!

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

########## 3. PduRReduceConstantData2Define

- **UUID**: `98612aac-e295-4285-98e6-d3e53f113d84`
- **参数名**: `PduRReduceConstantData2Define`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM arrays as constant define.

FALSE: ROM arrays are generated as data even if all values are identical.
TRUE: ROM arrays are generated as constant define if all values are identical.

########## 4. PduRDeduplicateIndirectedData

- **UUID**: `ea82f6fd-26f7-4875-9ff2-7e2bbe29c907`
- **参数名**: `PduRDeduplicateIndirectedData`

- **符号名值**: false

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress 0:N relational ROM data in all CONFIG-CLASSES without increasing the runtime.
This option can be used in lib builds and in postbuild configurations.

TRUE: 0:N relational ROM data is compressed without decreasing the runtime.
Code: no change expected.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE

FALSE: symbol names are generated in a human readable style based on the MIP, tags and variant names.
TRUE: symbol names are generated based on the MIP and a CRC32.

########## 7. PduRInterfacesForDeactivatedData

- **UUID**: `078d413f-0285-4c16-98f7-f390515820d4`
- **参数名**: `PduRInterfacesForDeactivatedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate bsw data interfaces for deactivated data elements. This is an advantage for the BSW developer to reduce the time to market with a development environment using auto completition and to investigate potential interfaces.

FALSE: data interfaces are not generated if the data elementis deactivated.
TRUE: data interfaces are generated as e.g. emty macros.

########## 8. PduRReferringKeysInComments

- **UUID**: `24695715-99a9-41f8-a1af-8657fb16f5f0`
- **参数名**: `PduRReferringKeysInComments`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE

FALSE: referring keys are not generated in comments.
TRUE: referring keys are generated in comments.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. PduROutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy

- **UUID**: `59ecd4c4-0c6c-43a2-8308-7625f7d7b5de`
- **参数名**: `PduROutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_SATURATION` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_CHECKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to configure a strategy to protect the code to write out of bounds.

NONE: no protection strategy is generated in the data access.
INDEX_SATURATION: arrays are blown up and the data access index is saturated by an appropriate mask. The advantage is the speed of the data access, but own data elements at other indexes of the same variable can be overridden. This debugging feature must not be used in production code!
INDEX_CHECKING: the data access index is validated by a runtime check. The advantage is that values are never written to incorrect indexes of the data access. This debugging feature must not be used in production code!

########## 2. PduRNumericalDataTypeMinimizationStrategy

- **UUID**: `f981ef7b-4fc3-425b-8ddb-d06c89193bfa`
- **参数名**: `PduRNumericalDataTypeMinimizationStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `WITH_CAST`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITHOUT_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITH_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to minimize the datatypes of CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE numerical data based on the used values.

NONE: the datatype is not minimized.
WITHOUT_CAST: the datatype of numerical data is minimized. Unsigned data types are not changed to signed datatypes.
WITH_CAST: the datatype of numerical data is minimized. Unsigned data types can be optimized to signed datatypes.

########## 3. PduRBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy

- **UUID**: `b900a99d-d87d-4866-9adf-1c1b201f9cb5`
- **参数名**: `PduRBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `BITMASKING`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BOOLEAN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITFIELD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITMASKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to tailor the usage of boolean data in structures in all CONFIG-CLASSES. The difference between BITFIELD and BITMASKING depends on your compiler options and memory mapping.

BOOLEAN: The datatype of boolean data is native boolean.
Code: the code size is small.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is large.
Runtime: the runtime is fast.

BITFIELD: The bitfield type is used and the compiler extracts the boolean data from structures.
Code: the code size is larger than using BOOLEAN.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is smaller than using BOOLEAN.
Runtime: the runtime is larger than using BOOLEAN.

BITMASKING: Generated Masks are used to extract the boolean data from structures.
Code: the code size is larger than using BOOLEAN.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is smaller than using BOOLEAN.
Runtime: the runtime is larger than using BOOLEAN.

########## 4. PduRDataDeduplicationStrategy

- **UUID**: `7ec70605-6fb4-472a-99e0-9ffe91298734`
- **参数名**: `PduRDataDeduplicationStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `WITH_CAST`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITHOUT_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITH_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to deduplicate CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE  ROM data.

NONE: The generated data is not deduplicated.

WITHOUT_CAST: The data is deduplicated without using casts.
Code: no change expected.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size can be minimized.
Runtime: no change expected.

WITH_CAST: The data is deduplicated using casts.
Code: no change expected.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size can be minimized more than in DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST.
Runtime: no change expected.

########## 5. PduRReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy

- **UUID**: `583311d5-c679-4716-9a80-af75463f95cc`
- **参数名**: `PduRReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `WITH_ANY_VALUE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITH_ZERO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITH_ANY_VALUE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data deduplication mechaisms. A comparison with 0 is very efficient, but a numerical comparison with a value not 0 can be used to increase the ROM data compression rate.

NONE: ROM data deduplications are switched off.
Code: the code size is small.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is large.
Runtime: the runtime is fast.

WITH_ZERO: ROM data deduplications can be applied with the value 0.
Code: the code size is larger than using NONE
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is smaller than using NONE.
Runtime: the runtime is larger than using NONE.

WITH_ANY_VALUE: ROM data deduplications can be applied with any numerical value.
Code: the code size is larger than using NONE
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is smaller than using DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO.
Runtime: the runtime is larger than using NONE.

- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate bsw data interfaces for deactivated data elements. This is an advantage for the BSW developer to reduce the time to market with a development environment using auto completition and to investigate potential interfaces.

FALSE: data interfaces are not generated if the data elementis deactivated.
TRUE: data interfaces are generated as e.g. emty macros.

######## 8. PduRReferringKeysInComments

- **UUID**: `24695715-99a9-41f8-a1af-8657fb16f5f0`
- **参数名**: `PduRReferringKeysInComments`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate referring keys in comments. This is an advantage for the developer to investigate indirections, but this feature reduces the overall readability of the generated data.

FALSE: referring keys are not generated in comments.
TRUE: referring keys are generated in comments.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. PduROutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy

- **UUID**: `59ecd4c4-0c6c-43a2-8308-7625f7d7b5de`
- **参数名**: `PduROutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_SATURATION` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_CHECKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to configure a strategy to protect the code to write out of bounds.

NONE: no protection strategy is generated in the data access.
INDEX_SATURATION: arrays are blown up and the data access index is saturated by an appropriate mask. The advantage is the speed of the data access, but own data elements at other indexes of the same variable can be overridden. This debugging feature must not be used in production code!
INDEX_CHECKING: the data access index is validated by a runtime check. The advantage is that values are never written to incorrect indexes of the data access. This debugging feature must not be used in production code!

######## 2. PduRNumericalDataTypeMinimizationStrategy

- **UUID**: `f981ef7b-4fc3-425b-8ddb-d06c89193bfa`
- **参数名**: `PduRNumericalDataTypeMinimizationStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `WITH_CAST`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITHOUT_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITH_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to minimize the datatypes of CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE numerical data based on the used values.

NONE: the datatype is not minimized.
WITHOUT_CAST: the datatype of numerical data is minimized. Unsigned data types are not changed to signed datatypes.
WITH_CAST: the datatype of numerical data is minimized. Unsigned data types can be optimized to signed datatypes.

######## 3. PduRBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy

- **UUID**: `b900a99d-d87d-4866-9adf-1c1b201f9cb5`
- **参数名**: `PduRBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `BITMASKING`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BOOLEAN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITFIELD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITMASKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to tailor the usage of boolean data in structures in all CONFIG-CLASSES. The difference between BITFIELD and BITMASKING depends on your compiler options and memory mapping.

BOOLEAN: The datatype of boolean data is native boolean.
Code: the code size is small.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is large.
Runtime: the runtime is fast.

BITFIELD: The bitfield type is used and the compiler extracts the boolean data from structures.
Code: the code size is larger than using BOOLEAN.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is smaller than using BOOLEAN.
Runtime: the runtime is larger than using BOOLEAN.

BITMASKING: Generated Masks are used to extract the boolean data from structures.
Code: the code size is larger than using BOOLEAN.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is smaller than using BOOLEAN.
Runtime: the runtime is larger than using BOOLEAN.

######## 4. PduRDataDeduplicationStrategy

- **UUID**: `7ec70605-6fb4-472a-99e0-9ffe91298734`
- **参数名**: `PduRDataDeduplicationStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `WITH_CAST`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITHOUT_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITH_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to deduplicate CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE  ROM data.

NONE: The generated data is not deduplicated.

WITHOUT_CAST: The data is deduplicated without using casts.
Code: no change expected.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size can be minimized.
Runtime: no change expected.

WITH_CAST: The data is deduplicated using casts.
Code: no change expected.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size can be minimized more than in DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST.
Runtime: no change expected.

######## 5. PduRReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy

- **UUID**: `583311d5-c679-4716-9a80-af75463f95cc`
- **参数名**: `PduRReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `WITH_ANY_VALUE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITH_ZERO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `WITH_ANY_VALUE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data deduplication mechaisms. A comparison with 0 is very efficient, but a numerical comparison with a value not 0 can be used to increase the ROM data compression rate.

NONE: ROM data deduplications are switched off.
Code: the code size is small.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is large.
Runtime: the runtime is fast.

WITH_ZERO: ROM data deduplications can be applied with the value 0.
Code: the code size is larger than using NONE
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is smaller than using NONE.
Runtime: the runtime is larger than using NONE.

WITH_ANY_VALUE: ROM data deduplications can be applied with any numerical value.
Code: the code size is larger than using NONE
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is smaller than using DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO.
Runtime: the runtime is larger than using NONE.

####### 1. PduRRoutingPathGroup

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `350b65f4-e4e9-4e4a-91f7-d878ee37cd3d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRRoutingPathGroup`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

A routing path group is a group of PduRDestPdus that can be disabled and enabled during runtime. The group contains the PduRDestPdus and not the routing path itself. This way the destinations of a routing path can be enabled and disabled independently. Enabling and disabling of routing path groups is typically used by the BswM module.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. PduRRoutingPathGroupId

- **UUID**: `6e2b9ebf-0902-4abe-96c6-358611c7f906`
- **参数名**: `PduRRoutingPathGroupId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Identification of the routing group.This numerical Identifier will be calculate automatically. Use the generated symbolic value to enable/disable the group.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. PduRIsEnabledAtInit

- **UUID**: `8518aa2d-9e08-48cd-bec5-69337b3cc67e`
- **参数名**: `PduRIsEnabledAtInit`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If set to true this routing path group will be enabled after initializing the PDU Router module (i.e. enabled in the PduR_Init function). If set to false the routing path group will be disabled after initializing the PDU Router module

############## 3. PduRDestPduRoutingType

- **UUID**: `685ed1f3-ac9e-4fb8-92ba-29c1d00f04d1`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduRoutingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNDEFINED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `API_FORWARDING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `GATEWAY_ROUTING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `UNDEFINED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter must never be changed manually. It is calculated automatically by the PduR validation.

PduR routing path destination Pdu routing type.

The routing type determines the routing operation mode of the routing path:
API_FORWARDING: Routing between a lower layer and an upper layer module.
GATEWAY_ROUTING: Routing between a lower layer module(s).
UNDEFINED: The routing type cannot be determined.

############## 4. PduRDestPduDataProcessing

- **UUID**: `909b0287-a1c8-4868-b67c-ff48c4f4a844`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduDataProcessing`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `IMMEDIATE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEFERRED`
  - `IMMEDIATE`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This option determines in which context the routing to the destination module is performed. 
IMMEDIATE: The routing is performed in either transmit or indication function.
DEFERRED: The routing is performed in the context of a main function.

############## 5. PduRPduLengthHandlingStrategy

- **UUID**: `6de4ccd0-2c84-451c-9f7a-ab684ede91e0`
- **参数名**: `PduRPduLengthHandlingStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNUSED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `UNUSED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `SHORTEN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DISCARD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `IGNORE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter is only used for communication interface routings. It is ignored for transport protocol routings.
UNUSED: use this value for Tp routing paths or unqueued If routing paths
SHORTEN: the If Pdu is shortened to the configured global Pdu length if a larger Pdu is received at the source (default value from previous PduR versions)
DISCARD: the If Pdu is discarded if a larger Pdu than configured is received at the source
IGNORE: the If Pdu length is ignored and handed over to the Destination modules. (only for unqueued IF Routing paths)

############ 2. PduRTpThreshold

- **UUID**: `a818fffc-0e3f-4506-bab9-de67a9c16e76`
- **参数名**: `PduRTpThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the number of bytes which shall be received before transmission on the destination shall be triggered (DestModule_Transmit is called).
It is only relevant for queued transport protocol routing paths. This parameter is only used for derived routing paths due to compatibility reasons. 
It is sychronized to the corresponding /MICROSAR/PduR/PduRRoutingTables/PduRQueue/PduRSharedBufferQueue/PduRTpThreshold parameter.

############ 1. PduRTransmissionConfirmation

- **UUID**: `0b706a1f-670d-493d-b8c9-2a20771e08f9`
- **参数名**: `PduRTransmissionConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]

- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE

This parameter is only relevant for communication interface routing paths. Transport protocol modules will always call the TxConfirmation API.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. PduRDestPduDataProvision

- **UUID**: `0d7d24c7-8a6a-4dbd-ab8e-982b4444e298`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduDataProvision`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `PDUR_DIRECT`
  - `PDUR_TRIGGERTRANSMIT`
- **实现配置类**:

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies how data is provided: direct (as part of the Transmit call) or via the TriggerTransmit callback function after the transmission request. Only required for communication interface Tx DestPdus.

############ 2. PduRDestPduDirection

- **UUID**: `f28404f6-cdff-49dc-966f-e160650db5a4`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduDirection`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNDEFINED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `RECEIVE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `TRANSMIT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `UNDEFINED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter must never be changed manually. It is calculated automatically by the PduR validation.

PduR routing path destination Pdu communication direction.

RECEIVE: The routing path destination is a reception Pdu.
TRANSMIT: The routing path destination is a transmission Pdu.
UNDEFINED: The communication direction cannot be determined.

############ 3. PduRDestPduRoutingType

- **UUID**: `685ed1f3-ac9e-4fb8-92ba-29c1d00f04d1`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduRoutingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNDEFINED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `API_FORWARDING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `GATEWAY_ROUTING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `UNDEFINED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter must never be changed manually. It is calculated automatically by the PduR validation.

PduR routing path destination Pdu routing type.

The routing type determines the routing operation mode of the routing path:
API_FORWARDING: Routing between a lower layer and an upper layer module.
GATEWAY_ROUTING: Routing between a lower layer module(s).
UNDEFINED: The routing type cannot be determined.

############ 4. PduRDestPduDataProcessing

- **UUID**: `909b0287-a1c8-4868-b67c-ff48c4f4a844`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduDataProcessing`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `IMMEDIATE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEFERRED`
  - `IMMEDIATE`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This option determines in which context the routing to the destination module is performed. 
IMMEDIATE: The routing is performed in either transmit or indication function.
DEFERRED: The routing is performed in the context of a main function.

############ 5. PduRPduLengthHandlingStrategy

- **UUID**: `6de4ccd0-2c84-451c-9f7a-ab684ede91e0`
- **参数名**: `PduRPduLengthHandlingStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNUSED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `UNUSED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `SHORTEN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DISCARD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `IGNORE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter is only used for communication interface routings. It is ignored for transport protocol routings.
UNUSED: use this value for Tp routing paths or unqueued If routing paths
SHORTEN: the If Pdu is shortened to the configured global Pdu length if a larger Pdu is received at the source (default value from previous PduR versions)
DISCARD: the If Pdu is discarded if a larger Pdu than configured is received at the source
IGNORE: the If Pdu length is ignored and handed over to the Destination modules. (only for unqueued IF Routing paths)

- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNDEFINED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false

######### 1. PduRDestPdu

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `4058cdf7-c170-4471-b98c-b50beb393233`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRDestPdu`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container specifies one destination for the PDU to be routed.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **9** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. PduRDestPduHandleId

- **UUID**: `d407c13d-ce27-4518-8597-75595563c2d9`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduHandleId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter must never be changed manually. It is calculated automatically by the PduR validation.

PDU identifier assigned by PDU Router. Used by communication interface and transport protocol modules for confirmation and trigger transmit API calls

############ 2. PduRTpThreshold

- **UUID**: `a818fffc-0e3f-4506-bab9-de67a9c16e76`
- **参数名**: `PduRTpThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the number of bytes which shall be received before transmission on the destination shall be triggered (DestModule_Transmit is called).
It is only relevant for queued transport protocol routing paths. This parameter is only used for derived routing paths due to compatibility reasons. 
It is sychronized to the corresponding /MICROSAR/PduR/PduRRoutingTables/PduRQueue/PduRSharedBufferQueue/PduRTpThreshold parameter.

############ 3. PduRDestPduQueueDepth

- **UUID**: `0db05335-a3b6-415a-bb18-a730205f59c6`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduQueueDepth`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..0]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter is deprecated and can't be used anymore.

The parameter was moved to the corresponding /MICROSAR/PduR/PduRRoutingTables/PduRQueue container which shall be referenced by this PduRDestPdu.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. PduRTransmissionConfirmation

- **UUID**: `0b706a1f-670d-493d-b8c9-2a20771e08f9`
- **参数名**: `PduRTransmissionConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
- **描述**: Specifies if the destination BSW module will call the TxConfirmation API.

This parameter is only relevant for communication interface routing paths. Transport protocol modules will always call the TxConfirmation API.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. PduRDestPduDataProvision

- **UUID**: `0d7d24c7-8a6a-4dbd-ab8e-982b4444e298`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduDataProvision`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `PDUR_DIRECT`
  - `PDUR_TRIGGERTRANSMIT`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies how data is provided: direct (as part of the Transmit call) or via the TriggerTransmit callback function after the transmission request. Only required for communication interface Tx DestPdus.

############ 2. PduRDestPduDirection

- **UUID**: `f28404f6-cdff-49dc-966f-e160650db5a4`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduDirection`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNDEFINED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `RECEIVE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `TRANSMIT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `UNDEFINED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter must never be changed manually. It is calculated automatically by the PduR validation.

PduR routing path destination Pdu communication direction.

RECEIVE: The routing path destination is a reception Pdu.
TRANSMIT: The routing path destination is a transmission Pdu.
UNDEFINED: The communication direction cannot be determined.

############ 3. PduRDestPduRoutingType

- **UUID**: `685ed1f3-ac9e-4fb8-92ba-29c1d00f04d1`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduRoutingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNDEFINED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `API_FORWARDING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `GATEWAY_ROUTING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `UNDEFINED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter must never be changed manually. It is calculated automatically by the PduR validation.

PduR routing path destination Pdu routing type.

The routing type determines the routing operation mode of the routing path:
API_FORWARDING: Routing between a lower layer and an upper layer module.
GATEWAY_ROUTING: Routing between a lower layer module(s).
UNDEFINED: The routing type cannot be determined.

############ 4. PduRDestPduDataProcessing

- **UUID**: `909b0287-a1c8-4868-b67c-ff48c4f4a844`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduDataProcessing`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `IMMEDIATE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEFERRED`
  - `IMMEDIATE`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This option determines in which context the routing to the destination module is performed. 
IMMEDIATE: The routing is performed in either transmit or indication function.
DEFERRED: The routing is performed in the context of a main function.

############ 5. PduRPduLengthHandlingStrategy

- **UUID**: `6de4ccd0-2c84-451c-9f7a-ab684ede91e0`
- **参数名**: `PduRPduLengthHandlingStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNUSED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `UNUSED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `SHORTEN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DISCARD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `IGNORE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter is only used for communication interface routings. It is ignored for transport protocol routings.
UNUSED: use this value for Tp routing paths or unqueued If routing paths
SHORTEN: the If Pdu is shortened to the configured global Pdu length if a larger Pdu is received at the source (default value from previous PduR versions)
DISCARD: the If Pdu is discarded if a larger Pdu than configured is received at the source
IGNORE: the If Pdu length is ignored and handed over to the Destination modules. (only for unqueued IF Routing paths)

PDU identifier assigned by PDU Router. Used by communication interface and transport protocol modules for confirmation and trigger transmit API calls

########## 2. PduRTpThreshold

- **UUID**: `a818fffc-0e3f-4506-bab9-de67a9c16e76`
- **参数名**: `PduRTpThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the number of bytes which shall be received before transmission on the destination shall be triggered (DestModule_Transmit is called).
It is only relevant for queued transport protocol routing paths. This parameter is only used for derived routing paths due to compatibility reasons. 
It is sychronized to the corresponding /MICROSAR/PduR/PduRRoutingTables/PduRQueue/PduRSharedBufferQueue/PduRTpThreshold parameter.

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. PduRTransmissionConfirmation

- **UUID**: `0b706a1f-670d-493d-b8c9-2a20771e08f9`
- **参数名**: `PduRTransmissionConfirmation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE

- **描述**: Specifies if the destination BSW module will call the TxConfirmation API.

This parameter is only relevant for communication interface routing paths. Transport protocol modules will always call the TxConfirmation API.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. PduRDestPduDataProvision

- **UUID**: `0d7d24c7-8a6a-4dbd-ab8e-982b4444e298`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduDataProvision`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC

- **可选值**:

  - `PDUR_TRIGGERTRANSMIT`

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies how data is provided: direct (as part of the Transmit call) or via the TriggerTransmit callback function after the transmission request. Only required for communication interface Tx DestPdus.

########## 2. PduRDestPduDirection

- **UUID**: `f28404f6-cdff-49dc-966f-e160650db5a4`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduDirection`

  - `TRANSMIT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `UNDEFINED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE

PduR routing path destination Pdu communication direction.

RECEIVE: The routing path destination is a reception Pdu.
TRANSMIT: The routing path destination is a transmission Pdu.
UNDEFINED: The communication direction cannot be determined.

########## 3. PduRDestPduRoutingType

- **UUID**: `685ed1f3-ac9e-4fb8-92ba-29c1d00f04d1`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduRoutingType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNDEFINED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `API_FORWARDING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `GATEWAY_ROUTING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `UNDEFINED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter must never be changed manually. It is calculated automatically by the PduR validation.

PduR routing path destination Pdu routing type.

The routing type determines the routing operation mode of the routing path:
API_FORWARDING: Routing between a lower layer and an upper layer module.
GATEWAY_ROUTING: Routing between a lower layer module(s).
UNDEFINED: The routing type cannot be determined.

########## 4. PduRDestPduDataProcessing

- **UUID**: `909b0287-a1c8-4868-b67c-ff48c4f4a844`
- **参数名**: `PduRDestPduDataProcessing`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `IMMEDIATE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEFERRED`
  - `IMMEDIATE`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This option determines in which context the routing to the destination module is performed. 
IMMEDIATE: The routing is performed in either transmit or indication function.
DEFERRED: The routing is performed in the context of a main function.

########## 5. PduRPduLengthHandlingStrategy

- **UUID**: `6de4ccd0-2c84-451c-9f7a-ab684ede91e0`
- **参数名**: `PduRPduLengthHandlingStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UNUSED`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `UNUSED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `SHORTEN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DISCARD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `IGNORE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter is only used for communication interface routings. It is ignored for transport protocol routings.
UNUSED: use this value for Tp routing paths or unqueued If routing paths
SHORTEN: the If Pdu is shortened to the configured global Pdu length if a larger Pdu is received at the source (default value from previous PduR versions)
DISCARD: the If Pdu is discarded if a larger Pdu than configured is received at the source
IGNORE: the If Pdu length is ignored and handed over to the Destination modules. (only for unqueued IF Routing paths)

########### 2. PduRSaTaSwitchingStrategy

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `4ffc15f8-150c-41e1-a692-29671faa343a`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRSaTaSwitchingStrategy`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

Switching strategy based on a source and target address.

- Learn source address location (PduRConnection) from every received Pdu
- Broadcast the received Pdu if the target address location is not yet known
- Route the received Pdu to the learned location (PduRConnection) if the target address is already known

############ 配置容器

本模块包含 **3** 个配置容器：

############# 1. PduRLinearTaToSaCalculationStrategy

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `b53dcda5-94ec-455a-9338-6348014ab18a`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRLinearTaToSaCalculationStrategy`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

The target address is the CAN ID of the received Pdu.
The source address is calculated by adding/subtracting a offset to target address and application of a bit mask.

Calculation:
for Pdus referenced by PduRAddOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address + offset) & mask
for Pdus referenced by PduRSubtractOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address - offset) & mask

############## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

############### INTEGER 类型参数

################ 1. PduROffset

- **UUID**: `f9293e1b-330f-45f0-b4df-c5b5e6f3bb40`
- **参数名**: `PduROffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Offset added to the target address.

################ 2. PduRMask

- **UUID**: `fc922421-752d-4102-b2d0-ceaed565f5df`
- **参数名**: `PduRMask`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `255`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Bit mask applied to the meta data of the received Pdus.

############# 3. PduRSaTaCalculationStrategy

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `3925972a-85ea-4cd0-8810-f818ac1df3b8`
- **容器类型**: CHOICE-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRSaTaCalculationStrategy`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器选择**:

- **PduRLinearTaToSaCalculationStrategy**
  - UUID: `b53dcda5-94ec-455a-9338-6348014ab18a`
  - 描述: The target address is the CAN ID of the received Pdu.
The source address is calculated by adding/subtracting a offset to target address and application of a bit mask.

Calculation:
for Pdus referenced by PduRAddOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address + offset) & mask
for Pdus referenced by PduRSubtractOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address - offset) & mask
- **PduRSaTaFromMetaDataCalculationStrategy**
  - UUID: `4611d32c-8527-41fc-befd-5e41ba1d856e`
  - 描述: The source- and target address are determined from the Pdu meta data (e.g. the CAN ID) based on the configured start- and end bit positions.

**容器描述**:

Strategy used to determine the source- and target address of the Pdus.

########### 3. PduRLinearTaToSaCalculationStrategy

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `b53dcda5-94ec-455a-9338-6348014ab18a`

- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

The target address is the CAN ID of the received Pdu.
The source address is calculated by adding/subtracting a offset to target address and application of a bit mask.

Calculation:
for Pdus referenced by PduRAddOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address + offset) & mask
for Pdus referenced by PduRSubtractOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address - offset) & mask

########### 3. PduRSaTaCalculationStrategy

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `3925972a-85ea-4cd0-8810-f818ac1df3b8`
- **容器类型**: CHOICE-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRSaTaCalculationStrategy`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器选择**:

- **PduRLinearTaToSaCalculationStrategy**
  - UUID: `b53dcda5-94ec-455a-9338-6348014ab18a`
  - 描述: The target address is the CAN ID of the received Pdu.
The source address is calculated by adding/subtracting a offset to target address and application of a bit mask.

Calculation:
for Pdus referenced by PduRAddOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address + offset) & mask
for Pdus referenced by PduRSubtractOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address - offset) & mask
- **PduRSaTaFromMetaDataCalculationStrategy**
  - UUID: `4611d32c-8527-41fc-befd-5e41ba1d856e`
  - 描述: The source- and target address are determined from the Pdu meta data (e.g. the CAN ID) based on the configured start- and end bit positions.

**容器描述**:

Strategy used to determine the source- and target address of the Pdus.

######### 4. PduRLinearTaToSaCalculationStrategy

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `b53dcda5-94ec-455a-9338-6348014ab18a`

- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

The target address is the CAN ID of the received Pdu.
The source address is calculated by adding/subtracting a offset to target address and application of a bit mask.

Calculation:
for Pdus referenced by PduRAddOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address + offset) & mask
for Pdus referenced by PduRSubtractOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address - offset) & mask

####### 13. PduRDataPlane

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `10a2e53f-f600-4e7f-8db7-695054ee60aa`

- **容器名称**: `PduRDataPlane`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器描述**:

This container specifies the behavior of an PduR data plane. A data plane is used for dynamic learning of routing relations between communication sources (e.g. external testers) and communication destinations (e.g. diagnosed onboard ECUs).

########### 3. PduRSaTaCalculationStrategy

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `3925972a-85ea-4cd0-8810-f818ac1df3b8`
- **容器类型**: CHOICE-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRSaTaCalculationStrategy`

**容器选择**:

- **PduRLinearTaToSaCalculationStrategy**
  - UUID: `b53dcda5-94ec-455a-9338-6348014ab18a`
  - 描述: The target address is the CAN ID of the received Pdu.
The source address is calculated by adding/subtracting a offset to target address and application of a bit mask.

Calculation:
for Pdus referenced by PduRAddOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address + offset) & mask
for Pdus referenced by PduRSubtractOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address - offset) & mask
- **PduRSaTaFromMetaDataCalculationStrategy**
  - UUID: `4611d32c-8527-41fc-befd-5e41ba1d856e`
  - 描述: The source- and target address are determined from the Pdu meta data (e.g. the CAN ID) based on the configured start- and end bit positions.

**容器描述**:

Strategy used to determine the source- and target address of the Pdus.

- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRLinearTaToSaCalculationStrategy`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

The target address is the CAN ID of the received Pdu.
The source address is calculated by adding/subtracting a offset to target address and application of a bit mask.

Calculation:
for Pdus referenced by PduRAddOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address + offset) & mask
for Pdus referenced by PduRSubtractOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address - offset) & mask

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. PduROffset

- **UUID**: `f9293e1b-330f-45f0-b4df-c5b5e6f3bb40`

- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Offset added to the target address.

########## 2. PduRMask

- **UUID**: `fc922421-752d-4102-b2d0-ceaed565f5df`
- **参数名**: `PduRMask`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `255`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Bit mask applied to the meta data of the received Pdus.

####### 17. PduRSaTaFromMetaDataCalculationStrategy

####### 20. PduRSingleBuffer

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `ab1ead95-97ad-439d-9b62-0e3506202e6a`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRSingleBuffer`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

A single buffer can only be used for Communication Interface routings with TriggerTransmit data provision. It can be used for a last-is-best use case. The Pdu in the queue will always be overwritten. The destination can read the queue and will always get the latest data.

####### 21. PduRSharedBufferQueue

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `70037054-94fd-4bfd-b6d8-a4eb49e3dbc4`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRSharedBufferQueue`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

This queue can be used for both communication interface and transport protocol routing paths. It has a First-In-First-Out behaviour (FIFO).
The actual memory used by this queue must be reserved using PduRTxBuffer container. These will be used by queues of this type.

A PduRTxBuffer can either be referenced explicitly by the corresponding parameter or it can be used implicitly by not referencing any PduRTxBuffer.
One PduRTxBuffer can either be allocated by all queues which explicitly reference this buffer or by all queues which do not reference any buffer.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. PduRQueueDepth

- **UUID**: `e13e860d-0e1f-48ff-98a9-11875cf01cd0`
- **参数名**: `PduRQueueDepth`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines how many Pdus can be stored in the queue.

########## 2. PduRTpThreshold

- **UUID**: `56aa845a-6f97-4e30-9717-025becb91114`
- **参数名**: `PduRTpThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter is only required if this queue is used by transport protocol routing paths.

Defines the number of bytes which shall be received before transmission on the destination shall be triggered (DestModule_Transmit is called).

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **3** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. PduRExplicitTxBufferRef

- **UUID**: `d4503ccb-860a-4930-9ef0-5d0424faed6c`
- **引用名**: `PduRExplicitTxBufferRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **目标**: `PduRTxBuffer`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/PduR/PduRRoutingTables/PduRTxBufferTable/PduRTxBuffer`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Reference to PduRTxBuffer. 
All referenced buffer will be used to enqueue a corresponding Pdu. A referenced buffer could also be referenced by other queues making it a shared buffer. The queues will compete for these shared buffer.
If there is no explicitly referenced buffer this queue will access all buffers which are not assigned to any queue. These are shown in the corresponding sibling parameter. This is also called global sharing of buffers.

########## 2. PduRImplicitTxBufferRef

- **UUID**: `998c15d3-d54f-4c1f-970d-65d5f4b60f41`
- **引用名**: `PduRImplicitTxBufferRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **目标**: `PduRTxBuffer`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/PduR/PduRRoutingTables/PduRTxBufferTable/PduRTxBuffer`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter must never be changed manually. It is calculated automatically by the PduR validation.

This parameter shows all globally shared PduRTxBuffer which are used by this queue. This is done for informational and validation purposes only.
If this queue uses explicitly assigned PduRTxBuffer than this parameter is not required and therefore deleted.

########## 3. PduRLockRef

- **UUID**: `8b57930c-6f08-4f37-8fb0-600986c67399`
- **引用名**: `PduRLockRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `PduRLock`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/PduR/PduRRoutingTables/PduRLock`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter must never be changed manually. It is calculated automatically by the PduR validation.

Reference to a PduRLock. This Lock is used to guard the PduR buffer allocation against concurrent access.
A reference is only required in a multicore usecase.

####### 22. PduRCommunicationInterfaceQueue

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `37a8593b-6712-488a-9e21-777f95fa2527`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRCommunicationInterfaceQueue`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

This queue can only be used for communication interface routing paths. It has a First-In-First-Out behaviour (FIFO).

The memory for this queue is automatically allocated. It is only used by this queue and can not be shared.
This dedicated memory results in much less overhead than in a shared buffer queue.
For small communication interface Pdus it is advised to use this queue (if FIFO behaviour is required) as buffer sharing results in a large overhead.

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines how many Pdus can be stored in the queue.

####### 23. PduRMulticoreQueue

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `0526d854-94b9-4303-bbc2-f06ee029b53f`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRMulticoreQueue`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

Multicore queue is used to perform the routing of communication interface routings between different applications.

Each container consists of two unidirectional multicore queues from application A to application B and vice versa.
Applications A and B can be a source and a destination at the same time. It is possible to specify the queue size for each direction independently.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Size of the multicore queue for communication from application A to application B.

########## 2. PduRMulticoreQueueSizeBtoA

####### 24. PduRSwitchingStrategy

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `08f0268f-39a5-4496-88bf-634b767f7846`
- **容器类型**: CHOICE-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRSwitchingStrategy`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: true

**容器选择**:

- **PduRSaTaSwitchingStrategy**
  - UUID: `4ffc15f8-150c-41e1-a692-29671faa343a`
  - 描述: Switching strategy based on a source and target address.

- Learn source address location (PduRConnection) from every received Pdu
- Broadcast the received Pdu if the target address location is not yet known
- Route the received Pdu to the learned location (PduRConnection) if the target address is already known
- **PduRLinearTaToSaCalculationStrategy**
  - UUID: `b53dcda5-94ec-455a-9338-6348014ab18a`
  - 描述: The target address is the CAN ID of the received Pdu.
The source address is calculated by adding/subtracting a offset to target address and application of a bit mask.

Calculation:
for Pdus referenced by PduRAddOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address + offset) & mask
for Pdus referenced by PduRSubtractOffsetSrcPduRef:
    source address = ( target address - offset) & mask
- **PduRSaTaFromMetaDataCalculationStrategy**
  - UUID: `4611d32c-8527-41fc-befd-5e41ba1d856e`
  - 描述: The source- and target address are determined from the Pdu meta data (e.g. the CAN ID) based on the configured start- and end bit positions.

**容器描述**:

Switching logic strategies.

  - UUID: `70037054-94fd-4bfd-b6d8-a4eb49e3dbc4`
  - 描述: This queue can be used for both communication interface and transport protocol routing paths. It has a First-In-First-Out behaviour (FIFO).
The actual memory used by this queue must be reserved using PduRTxBuffer container. These will be used by queues of this type.

A PduRTxBuffer can either be referenced explicitly by the corresponding parameter or it can be used implicitly by not referencing any PduRTxBuffer.
One PduRTxBuffer can either be allocated by all queues which explicitly reference this buffer or by all queues which do not reference any buffer.
- **PduRCommunicationInterfaceQueue**
  - UUID: `37a8593b-6712-488a-9e21-777f95fa2527`
  - 描述: This queue can only be used for communication interface routing paths. It has a First-In-First-Out behaviour (FIFO).

The memory for this queue is automatically allocated. It is only used by this queue and can not be shared.
This dedicated memory results in much less overhead than in a shared buffer queue.
For small communication interface Pdus it is advised to use this queue (if FIFO behaviour is required) as buffer sharing results in a large overhead.
- **PduRMulticoreQueue**
  - UUID: `0526d854-94b9-4303-bbc2-f06ee029b53f`
  - 描述: Multicore queue is used to perform the routing of communication interface routings between different applications.

Each container consists of two unidirectional multicore queues from application A to application B and vice versa.
Applications A and B can be a source and a destination at the same time. It is possible to specify the queue size for each direction independently.

**容器描述**:

Common configuration container for all supported queue variants of the PduR

##### 5. PduRRoutingPathGroup

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `350b65f4-e4e9-4e4a-91f7-d878ee37cd3d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `PduRRoutingPathGroup`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: true
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

A routing path group is a group of PduRDestPdus that can be disabled and enabled during runtime. The group contains the PduRDestPdus and not the routing path itself. This way the destinations of a routing path can be enabled and disabled independently. Enabling and disabling of routing path groups is typically used by the BswM module.

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Identification of the routing group.This numerical Identifier will be calculate automatically. Use the generated symbolic value to enable/disable the group.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. PduRIsEnabledAtInit

- **UUID**: `8518aa2d-9e08-48cd-bec5-69337b3cc67e`

- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If set to true this routing path group will be enabled after initializing the PDU Router module (i.e. enabled in the PduR_Init function). If set to false the routing path group will be disabled after initializing the PDU Router module

############ 2. PduRTpThreshold

- **UUID**: `a818fffc-0e3f-4506-bab9-de67a9c16e76`
- **参数名**: `PduRTpThreshold`

- **多重性**: [0..1]
